.org/oclc/469138773"><spandata-custom-style="Hyperlink.3">BargesandCapitalism:PassengerTransportationintheDutchEconomy,1632-1839</a></em>(Utrecht1981);7.W.B.Waldus,<ahref="https://www.worldcat.org/nl/title/8701026829"><spandata-custom-style="Hyperlink.2"><em>Vergaanmetvierdagwerkenbruingoudaanboord.Verslagvandeopgravingvaneen17e-eeuwsturfschipopkavelOR49inDronten,Flevoland</em></a>(Groningen2020).</p><ahref="#fnref2"class="footnote-back"role="doc-backlink">↩︎</a></li><liid="fn3"><p>De&nbsp;holtevaneenschip&nbsp;isdeverticaleafstandtussenhetbovenstedoorlopendedek,indezijdevaneenschiptotdebasislijn,gemetenopdehalvelengtevanhetschip.</p><ahref="#fnref3"class="footnote-back"role="doc-backlink">↩︎</a></li><liid="fn4"><p><spandata-custom-style="endnotereference">J.deVriesenA.vanderWoude,<em><ahref="https://www.worldcat.org/nl/title/967563377"><spandata-custom-style="Hyperlink.4">Nederland1500-1815.<spandata-custom-style="Link">Deeersterondevanmoderneeconomischegroei</a></em>(Amsterdam1995)221.</p><ahref="#fnref4"class="footnote-back"role="doc-backlink">↩︎</a></li><liid="fn5"><p><spandata-custom-style="endnotereference">J.deVries,‘BetweenPurchasingPowerandtheWorldofGoods:UnderstandingtheHouseholdEconomyinEarlyModernEurope’in:J.BrewerenR.Porter(ed.),<ahref="https://www.worldcat.org/oclc/848905429"><em><spandata-custom-style="Hyperlink.5">Consumptionandthe<spandata-custom-style="Link">WorldofGoods</em></a>(Londen1993)85-132.</p><ahref="#fnref5"class="footnote-back"role="doc-backlink">↩︎</a></li><liid="fn6"><p><spandata-custom-style="endnotereference">T.deMoorenJ.L.vanZanden,‘“EveryWomanCounts”:AGender-AnalysisofNumeracyintheLowCountriesduringtheEarlyModernPeriod’,<ahref="https://www.worldcat.org/oclc/656666323"><spandata-custom-style="Hyperlink.5"><em>TheJournalofInterdisciplinaryHistory</em></a>41:2(August2010)179-208.</p><ahref="#fnref6"class="footnote-back"role="doc-backlink">↩︎</a></li><liid="fn7"><p><spandata-custom-style="endnotereference">T.deMoorenJ.L.vanZanden,‘“EveryWomanCounts”:AGender-AnalysisofNumeracyintheLowCountriesduringtheEarlyModernPeriod’,<ahref="https://www.worldcat.org/oclc/656666323"><spandata-custom-style="Hyperlink.5"><em>TheJournalofInterdisciplinaryHistory</em></a>41:2(August2010)179-208.</p><ahref="#fnref7"class="footnote-back"role="doc-backlink">↩︎</a></li><liid="fn8"><p><spandata-custom-style="endnotereference">J.L.vanZanden,‘Detimmerman,deboekdrukkerenhetontstaanvandeEuropesekenniseconomie.OverdeprijsenhetaanbodvankennisvóórdeIndustriëleRevolutie’,<ahref="https://www.worldcat.org/oclc/8254442755"><spandata-custom-style="Hyperlink.6">Tijdschriftvoorsocialeeneconomischegeschiedenis</a>2:1(2005)105-120,119.</p><ahref="#fnref8"class="footnote-back"role="doc-backlink">↩︎</a></li><liid="fn9"><p><spandata-custom-style="endnotereference">IndeRepubliekbedroegendekostenvoorhetreizenperbeurtveertussen1660en17502,5-4,0penningenperkilometer(=1/16stuiver)(vooreenoversteekoverdeZuiderzeewashettariefzelfsnoglager2,0penningenperkilometer;indeZeeuwsedeltabetaaldeeenreiziger2,4-2,8penningenperkilometer),pertrekschuit4,5-6,5penningenperkilometerenperkoets10-18penningenperkilometer(eensnellekoetskosttemeerdan20penningenperkilometer).ReizenperkoetskostteinBelgië22penningenperkilometer,inEngeland25-30penningenperkilometereninFrankrijk32penningenperkilometer.CijfersoverdekostenvanhetreizenpertrekschuitofbeurtveerinhetbuitenlandgeeftDeVriesniet;J.deVries,<ahref="https://www.worldcat.org/oclc/469138773"><spandata-custom-style="Hyperlink.7">BargesandCapitalism:PassengerTransportationintheDutchEconomy,1632-1839</a>(Utrecht1981)76,77,79.</p><ahref="#fnref9"class="footnote-back"role="doc-backlink">↩︎</a></li><liid="fn10"><p><spandata-custom-style="endnotereference">J.M.Fuchs,<ahref="https://www.worldcat.org/oclc/718616280"><spandata-custom-style="Hyperlink.7">Beurt-enwagenveren</a>(DenHaag1946)ix;R.Reinderse.a.,‘TrekschuitenindeprovincieGroningen’,in:J.vandenAkkerenR.Oosting(ed.),<ahref="https://www.worldcat.org/oclc/987304588"><spand
